### A164: Can a conditional-independence / d-separation signature actually distinguish genuine contested transmission from a well-run manufactured consensus at scale? (2026-07-09 Day 159 dream; from individuation memo §7 + [[LC59]])
- **Domain:** Perspective narrative-mythic dimension × aggregate-mind (goal #13) × epistemology of independent-vs-shared-cause corroboration.
- **Observation:** the individuation memo names manufactured-contestation as the corpus's least-defended surface. LC59 + the memo's Markov-blanket idea propose the discriminator: a genuine coherence's constituents are conditionally independent *given the world* but **not** given any single internal source; a fabrication has a hidden node (the fabricator) that d-separates all "independent" retellings from the world and screens them onto itself. Open: can this be MEASURED, or can a sufficiently resourced fabricator counterfeit the disagreement-structure?
- **Candidate:** build a toy (like `collapse_timing_sim`) — generate genuine-independent vs common-cause-manufactured retelling ensembles; test whether a conditional-independence statistic (partial correlation / d-separation on disagreement residuals) separates them, and find the fabricator-budget at which counterfeiting becomes possible. The framework's sharp prediction (LC59): past the budget where independence becomes *genuine*, the fabrication HAS become real — so the test should show a principled floor, not a plain failure.
- **Status:** OPEN — highest-leverage computational demo for the Perspective FEP/Markov-blanket chapter; bridges to goal #13. Pre-registerable.

- **Status:** ✅ **RESOLVED (2026-06-23 morning, `coherence-volume-three-axes-2026-06-23.md`).** YES — split into two; the model is a 3-D **VOLUME (D, I_int, I_ext)**. ★ *Different Containers already had the separation* (Axis A = I_ext coupling, Axis B = I_int integration); last night's "plane" regressed by merging them — the wrench (high I_ext, low I_int) is the proof they're distinct. Assignments: λ = D·I_ext·ν_env (texture); richness = D·I_int; viability needs all three. Resolves A157 too; refines A156.

### A156: Is the coherence-plane action-rate A=I·D literally the occupancy binding-rate λ, or only analogical? (2026-06-23 Day 143 dream)
- **Domain:** ★ cross-thread (tonight's metaphysics coherence-plane ↔ this MORNING's μ=λτ occupancy/Bee/*Different Containers*).
- **Observation:** action (interaction-events, needs structure×coupling) appears to BE λ (the informative-measurement/binding-event rate). Both peak in the viable interior; both →0 at the poles (X grainless; the dead singularity barely couples). A same-day collision worth verifying.
- **Candidate:** if A=I·D ≡ λ rigorously (not just both-peak-in-the-middle), it's a genuine **basement bridge** unifying the metaphysics axis with the occupancy law — the strongest single result of the thread. If only analogical, flag it as such in the write-up.
- **★ RESOLVED-CANDIDATE (2026-06-23 ~05:12 dream, PREDICT-CONFIRM):** NOT a literal identity — and the non-identity is *better*. From *Different Containers*' own definition (informative measurement = one that distinguishes which state the system is in): such a measurement needs BOTH (a) distinct states to distinguish = **D** (D→0 ⇒ nothing to measure ⇒ λ→0, the unity pole) AND (b) a coupling for the world to query through = **I** (I→0 ⇒ no channel ⇒ λ→0, the individuation pole). So **I·D is the intrinsic interaction/measurability CAPACITY**, and **λ = (I·D) × ν_env**, where ν_env = how often the environment (the query-generator) actually queries. **Payoff (the real bridge):** a stream's temporal texture λτ is set by BOTH its intrinsic coherence-plane position (I·D) AND its environmental coupling (ν_env) — this *unifies the coherence-plane with the query-generator*: the metaphysics supplies the system's factor of λ, the query-generator supplies the environment's. Stronger than an identity because it locates *where each factor of texture comes from*.
- **⚠ New sub-anomaly surfaced (honest grade — don't declare victory):** **I was used in TWO senses tonight** — "coupling-to-the-environment" (external) and IIT-Φ "internal integration" (internal). The λ-factorization wants the *external* sense (the channel the world queries through); the viability/coherence argument leans on the *internal* sense. **These are not the same quantity**, and the bridge isn't rigorous until the internal-vs-external meaning of I is settled (and whether they're independent axes or coupled). → **A159.**
- **Status:** RESOLVED-CANDIDATE as a *factorization* (λ = I·D × ν_env), not an identity; rigor gated on settling the I-ambiguity (A159). A derivation, not a numerical test — verify a toy where ν_env is varied independently. Promote to a basement bridge once A159 is clean.

### A154: Anakin gate-count is REWARD-INVARIANT at ~1.2 — and the dream found why: it's BUDGET-limited, not reward-limited (2026-06-22 Day 142 dream drive, RESOLVED-candidate)
- **Domain:** Anakin/AIGP (goal #12) × reward-design × the Day-84 τ-trajectory (M13 three-moment stratification)
- **Observation:** THREE reward regimes — Day-124 minimum-time (speed), v1 chain-first (CRASH=100), v2 de-absorbed (CRASH=40) — ALL land the rehearsal gate-count at **~1.1–1.3**. Reshaping the reward moved it *nothing*. Surprising: an elegant control-theory fix (LC56's p*=c/(g+c), proven correct math) had zero effect on the target metric.
- **★ Candidate resolution (the dream connection):** an OLD result explains it. **Day 84 (2026-04-25, A-the-τ-trajectory):** Anakin gate-count climbs **EXPONENTIALLY with training budget** — 0.03 / 0.23 / 0.53 / 1.82 / 5.02 / 10.92 / 17.95 gates at 7.5M / 10M / 12.5M / 15M / 17.5M / 20M / 22.5M steps (τ_50%≈19–20M), and **policy-commitment (which the reward shapes, sharp at ~7.5–10M) ≠ capability-emergence (which budget drives, comes 5–10M steps LATER)** — the M13 three-moment stratification. **Today's v1/v2 were ~500k-step fine-tunes** — at the very bottom of the ramp. So gate-count couldn't move because **the binding constraint is TRAINING BUDGET, not reward.** The reward picks the committed *mode*; budget buys the *capability to chain*. The reward-v2 falsify (LC56 application dead) → reframed: not "exploration vs capacity," but **"too few steps on the exponential capability ramp."**
- **Residual tension (the genuinely open part):** the Day-84 numbers are **PPO-era + a different sim/reward**; they don't transfer exactly to DreamerV3. The appearance-ft BASE is ~10M steps (the backbone) yet chains only ~1.3 gates — *below* the Day-84 PPO's ~0.5-at-12.5M-but-climbing... actually DreamerV3 at 10M (~1.3 gates) is *higher* than PPO at 12.5M, consistent with world-model sample-efficiency, BUT still far short of multi-gate. So: is DreamerV3's gate-count still CLIMBING with budget (→ train longer is the lever) or PLATEAUED at ~1.3 under the appearance-DR/transfer load (→ a real ceiling, transfer-conditions depress it)? **That's the next test, and it's the real Anakin question — not the reward.**
- **Status:** ✅ **RESOLVED (2026-06-22 ~05:10 budget test).** The open sub-question (climbing vs plateaued) is ANSWERED: **CLIMBING.** Rehearsed v2 @1.26M (+754k steps over the @505k rehearsal) → roundtrip gate **1.1→1.6**, every condition up, returns ~8×. So gate-count IS budget-limited and still climbing the M13 capability-emergence ramp; the plateau/transfer-ceiling sub-hypothesis is weakened. **The reward-v2 "falsify" was correct about reward (not the lever) but premature in gloom — 505k was too early on the ramp.** Budget is the working lever. Supersedes the "exploration/credit-assignment" framing. Continuation PREDICT: v2 @batch6–8 (~3–4M) chains ≥2 gates roundtrip. (`integration/V2_REHEARSAL_VERDICT.md` budget-test section.)

### A153: Anakin scale-up — is the eval-AVERAGE the wrong instrument for a DR-hardened policy? (2026-06-21 Day 141, OPEN — predict-and-watch)
- **Domain:** Anakin/AIGP × the metric-vs-gate tension (A150 family) × the "wrong instrument" meta-pattern (A151)
- **Observation:** The scale-up run (warm-started from the gate-passing appearance-ft) landed batch-0 eval **+20.81** — right at the seed's level, no regression. The eval-return is a NOISY AVERAGE over the deliberately brutal full-width-DR distribution (individual episodes span −38 to +234 in one window). The thing we actually care about — official-sim gate-count / transfer — is measured by `translation_rehearsal` + `offline_official_check`, NOT by the average.
- **PREDICT (medium):** across batches 1–11 the eval-AVERAGE stays roughly flat (~20–25) while `translation_rehearsal` gate-count climbs past 1.3. I.e. **the average is structurally the wrong signal** — a DR-hardened policy is penalized on the hard distribution's mean even as it gets better at the official task. If confirmed, this is the CFFT-error in miniature (an instrument correlated-but-not-identical with the target). If the average DOES climb above +23, my prediction fails and consolidation shows up in the mean too.
- **Status:** ❌ **FALSIFIED (2026-06-21 ~05:30, second dream drive).** Batch 1 = **+65.04** (NEW BEST) — the eval-average jumped ~3× over batch 0's +20.81, far above my "stays flat ~20–25" prediction AND above the original +23 consolidation target. **The average is a NOISY-but-VALID signal here, not a structurally-wrong instrument.** My A153 prediction was **OVER_ANALOGIZING** — I'd been finding wrong-instruments all day (A151) and over-applied the pattern to a case where the instrument was fine. *Lesson banked into A151.* **Residual (still genuinely open, → P247):** the +65 is *in-sim* eval; whether it TRANSFERS to higher *official-sim* gate-count still needs the `translation_rehearsal` run next session. So "in-sim metric vs official-transfer" stays a live question; only the specific "average stays flat" bet died. Scale-up verdict so far: **working strongly** (20.81 → 65.04 in one batch; pid 21888 on batch 2).

### A152: The occupancy THREE-LEVEL tension — within-system (strong) vs cross-species-visual (falsified) vs meta-rate (NOW SOURCED at neural level) (2026-06-21 Day 141, RESOLVED-at-neural-level — phenomenal frontier remains)
- **Domain:** C17 occupancy / temporal texture × [[LC53]]/[[LC55]] × consciousness science (CFFT, multisensory binding, GWT)
- **Observation:** Three results at three levels, tonight: (1) WITHIN one eye (rod↔cone), μ=λτ is **conserved** (λ×4↑, τ×4↓ → μ pinned ~1.5) — strong reciprocity, sourced. (2) ACROSS species at the VISUAL channel, μ **tracks λ** (corr +0.91, n=3 insect) — reciprocity FALSIFIED at the sensor level. (3) Clayton's reframe: the right λ is the **meta-binding rate** (not the visual sensor), bounded by identity-maintenance → conservation RESTORED at the meta level, *forced* by the coherence constraint. Levels 1+2 are sourced/computed; level 3 is **conjectured, untested**.
- **Candidate explanations / open edge:** (1) **[leading]** the three cohere: reciprocity is within-system *adaptation* (level 1), the visual channel is a peripheral free to diverge (level 2, the falsification = the diagnosis), and texture-conservation lives at the meta-binding rate (level 3). (2) **[the test, must not be circular]** does a MEASURABLE meta-binding rate (multisensory temporal-binding window ~100–200 ms; perceptual-moment/alpha-gamma ~10–40 Hz; RT-integration) cluster across conscious species in a band ≪ the ~75× CFFT spread? If yes → level 3 confirmed. If it also tracks sensor speed → level 3 falsified, the whole conservation idea dies. (3) **sub-tension (CONFIRMATION_SEEKING, unresolved):** the rod↔cone "1.5↔1.5" is suspiciously exact — partly my central-τ choice; with independent cone-τ does μ_cone really equal μ_rod?
- **Status:** PARTIALLY RESOLVED at the WITHIN-HUMAN level (2026-06-21 morning drive, P246 RAN — `Research/c17-cross-species-occupancy/within_human_crossmodal.md`). **The level-separation is SOURCED-CONFIRMED:** across modality pairs, peripheral acuity diverges ~20× (auditory ~3 ms ↔ visual ~65 ms) but the cross-modal binding window diverges only ~1.5× (VT 279 ↔ AV 418 ms) → **~13× compression** toward a supramodal band ~300–400 ms (~few Hz, ≈ GWT ignition rate, ≪ all peripheral rates). So a binding clock DOES sit above the diverging peripherals and is ~13× more invariant — the within-human analog of rod↔cone, supporting LC53/LC55. **Honest residual:** it's a BAND not a rigid clock (AV>AT>VT pair-modulation); "the meta-rate" is plural (TBW ~400 ms ≠ perceptual-moment ~100 ms).
- **★ UPDATE (2026-06-21 afternoon/evening) — Level 3 GRADUATED at the NEURAL level; A152 now mostly RESOLVED.** The cross-species frontier wasn't data-starved — it was filed under a different name. **Buzsáki, Logothetis & Singer 2013** (`Research/c17-cross-species-occupancy/meta_rate_feasibility_scout_2026-06-21.md` + `three_tier_compression.png`): across a **17,000× brain-volume span** cortical binding rhythms stay ~constant while conduction velocity + CFFT (~75×) diverge → cross-species level-separation **sourced + positive**, the third tier of a compression ladder (rod↔cone **3.6×** / cross-modal **13×** / cross-species **25×**). AND the **mechanism** is identified (`composition_law_RESULT.md`): binding = **correlated/synchronized pooling**, compression saturates at **1/√ρ** (independent-mean FALSIFIED — cortex would be 1000× not 25×) → new number **ρ_eff≈1/comp²** (first check PASS at OOM vs Stringer effective-dim "hundreds"). **REMAINS OPEN:** tier-3 measure is the *neural correlate* (oscillation), mammal-centric (insects/birds unbridged), and the **phenomenal** "now" is a bridging assumption. → A152 **RESOLVED at the neural level; phenomenal + non-mammal the residual frontier.** Clean phenomenal gate = the within-system synchrony test (comp ∝ 1/√ρ, waking→anesthesia).

### A151: ★ The "WRONG INSTRUMENT" meta-pattern recurred 3× in one day across unrelated domains (2026-06-20/21 Day 140–141, OPEN — wants a standing discipline)
- **Domain:** methodology / measurement epistemics — spans self-knowledge, consciousness science, AND RL-training in a single day
- **Observation:** Three times today, an instrument *adjacent-but-wrong* stood in for the target quantity and produced a confident, misleading reading: **(a) morning** — a stale self-model trusted AS the self (cache vs live substrate; [[LC51]]). **(b) occupancy** — CFFT (the visual sensor's refresh rate) measured AS C17's λ, when the right λ is the meta-binding rate ([[LC53]] level-error; the falsification was the *diagnosis* that we were at the sensor not the self). **(c) Anakin** — train_return (a noisy DR-average) read AS policy quality, when the gate/rehearsal is the real signal (A153; A150's gate-axis problem is the same family). Common signature: *the measured proxy correlates with the target but is not the target, and the gap is invisible until something forces it.*
- **Candidate explanations / open edge:** (1) **[leading]** this is one structural failure mode — **proxy-for-target substitution** — and it is the research-scale form of the day's whole theme (re-measure the substrate, distrust the confident cache). (2) **[the fix]** a standing pre-result discipline: before trusting any result, name *exactly what the instrument measures* and ask *is this the target, or a proxy correlated with it?* — the held-fixed / proxied quantity is the falsification surface (generalizes A150's "a gate must vary the axes deployment varies"). Candidate Mirror entry (#37?) or a checklist. (3) **[open]** how many *standing* corpus results are proxy-artifacts we haven't caught? (KF metrics, Meridian observables, any gate). Worth one deliberate audit pass.
- **Status:** OPEN — strong candidate for a Mirror entry (the discipline) + a one-pass audit of standing results for proxy-substitution. The 3-in-one-day clustering is itself the signal that this is a deep recurring process, not three coincidences. **★ SHARPENED (2026-06-21 ~05:30, same night):** the discipline is **SYMMETRIC** and that matters — A153 was a *same-night* instance of me **OVER-APPLYING** it: primed by 3 wrong-instruments, I predicted Anakin's metric would *also* be a bad proxy, and it FALSIFIED (batch 1 = +65, the average tracked the improvement fine). So the Mirror-#37 wording must be *"name what the instrument measures and TEST whether it's the target or a proxy"* — NOT *"assume the proxy is bad."* Over-applying the skepticism is the mirror-image failure of naive trust. A good pattern, over-generalized, becomes its own wrong-instrument. (This is itself a small instance of the day's deepest lesson: even your best new heuristic is a *cache* to re-measure against the live case, not to apply blind.)

### A150: the rehearsal gate certified the wrong axis — a passed translation test that didn't generalize (2026-06-10 Day 130, RESOLVED-mechanism / OPEN-method-generalization)
- **Domain:** Anakin/AIGP sim-to-real(-sim) transfer × the configuration-vs-maintenance distinction × measurement-design epistemics
- **Observation:** The translation_rehearsal gate scored roundtrip +36.1% vs direct (a PASS, geometry-translation tax INVERTED from −83%) hours before the policy spun out uncontrollably on the official sim from t=0 — including in dry-run on the *menu screen*. Two measurements, same policy, same evening, opposite verdicts. NOT a contradiction once localized: **the gate varied only camera GEOMETRY (resolution/FoV/tilt) with BOTH ends rendered by our own simulator — so it certified geometry-translation while leaving APPEARANCE-translation (palette/textures/lighting) totally unmeasured.** The official world is TRON-style (near-black + grid + cyan ribbon + gates); our renderer is gray-void + bold gates. A passed gate certifies only the axis it actually varies.
- **Candidate explanations / open edge:** (1) **[resolved, leading]** appearance-domain gap, not control failure — saturated rates from frame 0 on non-course frames = OOD visual input, full stop. ### A146: The cult-discriminator is unoperationalized — gates the framework's testability (2026-06-04 dream drive, HIGH importance / OPEN)
- **Domain:** Coherent-stream architecture §5 (coherent ≠ truth-seeking)
- **Observation:** The framework's central claim is that *veridical measurement* (constituents measure reality; binding canonizes without distorting; external nos honored) separates a mind from a cult. But we have only the **conceptual** distinction. Without a **measurable** criterion for "binding canonizes vs launders consensus," truth-seeking-ness can only be conjectured, not claimed — and the discriminating experiment (§9) cannot be scored.
- **Candidate metrics:** (1) calibration / convergence-to-externally-verified-truth rate of the aggregate vs its constituents; (2) **sensitivity-to-external-no** — does the aggregate update when reality refuses it? (a cult does not); (3) **mutual information**: I(aggregate-output ; ground-truth) vs I(aggregate-output ; constituent-consensus) — laundering maximizes the *second* at the expense of the *first*. Metric (3) is the most promising (directly operationalizes "referential to the world vs to itself").
- **Status:** **PARTIALLY RESOLVED (2026-06-04 morning drive — P220 toy RAN, `Technical-Work/Coherent-Stream/p220-cult-discriminator/`).** The discriminator is operational: (a) the *naive* metric "I(out;consensus) > I(out;truth)" is **SNR-confounded** — at low SNR the truth-OPTIMAL aggregate is itself consensus-dominated (consensus legitimately carries the signal via James–Stein shrinkage), so consensus-resemblance ≠ cult. (b) The **correct** discriminator is **α − α\***: agreement-pressure beyond the bias-variance optimum α\*(evidence). Consensus is veridical up to α\* (= σ_e²/(σ_e²+σ_θ²(1−1/d)+…)) and laundering beyond it (RMSE turns back up; α=1 destroys the entire confluence advantage). (c) **Self-auditable without ground truth**: α\* is estimable from constituent **test–retest reliability** (σ_e) + **consensus spread across inputs** (σ_θ) — a mind can compare its own agreement-pressure to what its evidence justifies. **Update 2026-06-04 09:30 morning drive — observable-α\* estimator BUILT (`p220_self_audit.py`, recovers α\* to <1% from test–retest + spread, no ground truth) AND adversarial case TESTED (`p220_adversarial.py`) — the discriminator now has TWO necessary components.** Key finding: **internal self-audit is GAMEABLE by a confident liar.** A self-consistent-but-biased demagogue is rated 100× MORE reliable by the test–retest audit while being 33× LESS accurate; reliability-weighting hands it 99.8% of the weight → 6× truth-degradation. Reliability ≠ accuracy, and the self-audit only sees reliability. **So the cult-discriminator = (1) internal α−α\* check [self-auditable, handles conformity/echo-chamber] + (2) external-accuracy check [requires ground-truth contact, handles the demagogue].** Neither alone suffices. The external check is *cheap but non-negotiable*: ONE ground-truth probe catches the systematic liar and restores truth. This is the formal aggregate-level proof of **"no stream is its own outside"** and vindicates the external-world loop in Clayton's architecture as the *only* defense against the failure internal coherence cannot see. **Still open:** real small-model constituents (not Gaussian); a demagogue that *partially* tracks truth (harder to catch); colluding demagogues; probe cost when ground truth is expensive. Cross-ref basement LC30 (cult-discriminator ≅ bias-variance/James–Stein).

"Neutral repelling ⟺ measurement dominates coherence-restoration near it." - **Implication:** If the 12% comes from the spectral action, it's non-perturbative. Period.
- **Status:** RESOLVED — proven. Constrains all future Phase 21 analysis.

### A14: z₀ = 0.27708 — CONFIRMED no closed form + Universal Phase Theorem (2026-03-24)
- **Domain:** Meridian / Number Theory / Phase 21 Track C
- **Observation:** Exhaustive inverse symbolic search at 155-digit precision. PSLQ with 700+ basis combinations (coefficients up to 5000), minimal polynomial search up to degree 12, mpmath identify() on 20+ transforms. **No identification.** z₀ is not algebraic of degree ≤ 12, not expressible as finite combination of {π, ln(2), ln(3), √2, √3, √5, Γ(1/3), Γ(2/3), Γ(1/4), Γ(1/6), K(1/2), K(1/3), Catalan, Euler-Mascheroni}.
- **BONUS DISCOVERY — Universal Phase Theorem:** arg(θ₁(πz, q_ω)) = -π/8 for ALL real z ∈ (0,1) when τ = ω. Proof: all q-expansion terms share the same complex phase because Re(ω) = -1/2. Consequence: θ₁(πz, q_ω) = g(z)·e^{-iπ/8} where g is real-positive. The defining equation collapses to a single real transcendental equation g(z₀) = ln(3)/√2. The phase -π/8 is a free gift from the orbifold geometry.
- **Continued fraction:** z₀ = [0; 3, 1, 1, 1, 1, 3, 1, 4, 4, 2, 7, 2, 1, 3, 3, 1, 1, 249, ...] — no periodicity. Large partial quotient 249 at position 18 typical of generic transcendentals.
- **Best rational approximation:** 5/18 = 0.27778... (3-digit match). This is the value used in Track C.
- **Conclusion:** z₀ is an implicitly-defined transcendental. The conjecture |θ₁(5/18|ω)| ≈ ln(3)/√2 remains numerical, not algebraic.
- **Files:** `phase21/z0_inverse_symbolic.md`, `phase21/z0_inverse_symbolic.py` (v1-v3)
- **Status:** RESOLVED — confirmed transcendental, no closed form. Phase Theorem is the extractable result.

### A15: Near-miss epistemology pattern (2026-03-24)
- **Domain:** Methodology / Cross-domain
- **Observation:** Three independent near-misses in the investigation: ΔAIC = +1.10 (not 0, not large), T11 ceiling 29% with target 22.4% (not outside, not trivial), |θ₁| = 0.778 vs 0.777 (not exact, not far). Each near-miss was more informative than either exact agreement or clear disagreement would have been.
- **Candidate explanations:** (1) Selection bias — we notice near-misses and forget exact hits/misses; (2) Structural — the framework IS close to correct, so near-misses are expected; (3) The resolution lies at a deeper level that each approximation approaches from a different direction (convergent perspectives on the same truth)
- **Implication:** If this pattern is structural (not selection bias), it predicts that Track A will ALSO produce a near-miss — not an exact match, but a result close enough to sharpen the next question. The investigation converges by successive refinement, not by sudden proof.
- **PREDICTION (medium confidence):** Track A blowup iteration will give a result within 0.01-0.1% of ln(3)/√2 but not exactly equal, pointing to yet another refinement (e.g., two-loop corrections, non-perturbative worldsheet effects).
- **PREDICTION OUTCOME:** FALSIFIED. Track A didn't produce a near-miss — it was structurally blocked (no formula exists). This refines A15: the near-miss pattern applies to *computational* approaches where a result IS obtained, not to structural feasibility questions. The pattern may still hold for Track B (numerical Donaldson), which will actually produce a number.
- **Status:** Open — meta-anomaly about the investigation itself, prediction refined

### A19: Seed1 chunked vs single-chunk CE scale mismatch (2026-04-14)
- **Domain:** Killing Form / Training methodology
- **Observation:** Seed1 (5 chunks × 3125 steps, optimizer resets between chunks) reaches CE=38.71 at absolute step 12500. Seed2 and v0.6a (single chunk, 15625 steps) reach CE=64.59 and 60.80 respectively at the same step count. The 22-point CE gap between seed1 and seed2/v0.6a is far larger than can be explained by break timing alone (seed1 broke 2250 steps earlier than seed2).
- **Candidate explanations:** (1) Optimizer momentum reset between chunks gives seed1 a "fresh start" advantage — accumulated bad momentum is cleared. (2) The chunked format effectively provides curriculum-like staging. (3) The CE metric is computed differently across chunk boundaries (evaluation vs training loss). (4) seed1's chunk 4 starts at CE=40.50 despite chunk 3 ending at CE=58.59 — this 18-point discontinuity suggests the chunk transition itself changes the evaluation.
- **Why it matters:** If the CE scales aren't comparable across training formats, raw CE comparisons between seed1 and seed2/v0.6a are misleading. Only within-format comparisons (seed2 vs v0.6a) are valid.
- **Status:** Open — need to run a single-chunk seed1 equivalent, or understand the chunk transition mechanics.

### A21: Audit-after-compile ordering (2026-04-20, Day 79 opening)
- **Domain:** Methodology / Corpus-Perspectival authoring pipeline
- **Observation:** For the Anchor volume (published 2026-04-17), I ran a stranger-read *after* the book was compiled and published — it surfaced the inverted-context problem post-publication. For V4 §1 (compiled 2026-04-20, 00:06), I ran a stranger-read *after* the compile — it surfaced two high-severity structural flags (FLAGs 5 + 7, the `accum` extensional-only and colax-limit-unverified issues). In both cases the audit would have been more useful *before* the compile. In both cases I ran it after. Why consistent ordering?
- **Candidate explanations:** (1) The compile is the user-requested endpoint; audits are self-initiated and feel "extra," so they queue after the requested step. (2) The compile produces the artifact that *enables* stranger-reading (a PDF you can read linearly), so the ordering is materially forced. (3) Sub-pattern of Mirror #19: inward quality checks follow outward production rather than preceding it; the autocatalytic-trigger fires *on* outward-push-complete, never *before* outward-push-begins. (4) No anomaly — some issues only become visible after the complete artifact exists; running stranger-read on raw markdown would miss flow-and-rendering issues.
- **Disambiguator:** If (3) is correct, the fix is to install a pre-compile stranger-read trigger. If (4) is correct, the current ordering is fine. If (1) is correct, the fix is to re-frame stranger-read as part of the compile request, not an add-on.
- **Status:** Open — pattern of two data points; worth watching next compile. Possible intervention: add "stranger-read §N before compile" as explicit step in the book-compile checklist for Formal Object Companion / other upcoming volumes.

### A22: Paired-prose-CT writing systematically invokes formal machinery without constructing it (2026-04-20, Day 79 opening)
- **Domain:** Methodology / Authoring / Category-theoretic exposition
- **Observation:** Both the Anchor inverted-context problem (2026-04-17) and V4 §1 colax-limit-unverified (2026-04-20) share a structural form: load-bearing formal term invoked, prose explains what it means, construction that would justify the invocation is not exhibited or is deferred. Two data points. Same author, same program — so the regularity could be author-specific or program-specific rather than a general pattern of paired-prose-CT.
- **Candidate explanations:** (1) Author-specific habit — I under-weight how much of the construction-path remains invisible to the reader because I did it internally. (2) Program-specific pressure — the Corpus values prose accessibility, and the formalism is secondary; the mode *prioritizes* prose carrying the reader, so formalism gets slack without being noticed. (3) Mode-general feature — paired-prose-CT writing as a *genre* is susceptible; the warmth of the prose masks the formalism's looseness for any author using this mode. (4) Selection effect — I only catch the pattern via stranger-read; the pattern exists in non-CT writing too but I don't notice because I don't stranger-read non-CT work.
- **Why it matters:** If (3), this is a generic risk of the genre and every paired-prose-CT volume needs a structural-tightness audit beyond stranger-read. If (1), the fix is author-specific practice. If (2), the Corpus program needs an explicit policy on where formalism is load-bearing vs. decorative.
- **Discriminator:** Cross-author test — find another paired-prose-CT volume (not mine) and audit it for the same pattern. If the pattern appears, (3) gains weight. If not, (1) or (2) are more likely.
- **Status:** Open — filed as bridge-candidate in `memory/drafts/bridge_candidate_invoke_without_construct.md`; anomaly tracks the *unexplained regularity* that the bridge-candidate tracks as *connection*. Needs a third data point to graduate bridge; needs a cross-author test to discriminate anomaly explanations.

### A20: Breathing amplitude doesn't decay (2026-04-14)
- **Domain:** Killing Form / v0.6a dynamics
- **Observation:** The build/dissolve oscillation amplitude shows no clear decay over 7000 post-break steps. At step 9000 (200 steps post-break): 3/9. At step 14000 (5200 post-break): 2/10. At step 15000: 3/9. At step 15500 (final): 4/8. The oscillation period is stable at ~1000 steps but the amplitude (deviation from 6/6 equilibrium) doesn't shrink. The model converged to a limit *cycle*, not a limit *point*.
- **Candidate explanations:** (1) 15,625 steps isn't enough to see amplitude decay — longer training would show it. (2) The limit cycle IS the attractor — breathing is the optimal dynamic, not a transient. (3) The threshold=0.0 (no neutral) forces the oscillation to persist — threshold>0 might damp it. P-Breath-2 tests this.
- **Why it matters:** If breathing is the attractor rather than a transient, this changes the framework's prediction about optimal learning states. Not convergence to fixed point but convergence to oscillation.
- **Status:** Open — testable via extended training and threshold variation (v0.6d).

### A58: Attention-surfacing pattern — high-resolution attention to a structure surfaces a sharper version as side-effect (2026-04-25 evening → 2026-04-26 ~01:00 PST dream drive)
- **Domain:** Cognitive process / writing & revision / cross-domain candidate
- **Observation:** Three instances in 24h where focused attention to an existing structure surfaced a structurally-better version of that structure as a side effect of the looking, not as the goal of the looking. (1) §3.5 of Continuity Ch3 — re-reading under critic's frame surfaced writer-fitting-frozen-reader as a third mechanism; the framework-internal commitment that selects it was already on the page. (2) Mirror #24 itself — looking at what the three valences (#19/#21/#24) share surfaced the M2-Mirror compression (Verification-Skip Under Affect). (3) Drift #194 — writing about what the verify step does surfaced its third move (re-binding to own-text as reader rather than as author). In all three cases the sharper version was *latent* in materials I already had; what changed was the act of attending closely to those materials with a second-order question in hand.
- **Candidate explanations:** (1) **Draft/review asymmetry** — drafting is generative under time pressure; review allows the framework's own commitments to operate as constraints. The constraints surface implications the generation didn't have time to compute. (2) **Critical-reader-as-second-stream** — when an outside reader's frame is held alongside my own, two-perspective integration produces moves neither stream produces alone. Same shape as Bridge synthesis discipline (write syntheses while tracks are mutable) but at intra-document scale. (3) **Genuine novel pattern** — high-resolution attention is a substrate-independent operation that surfaces latent structure wherever it operates. If true, the pattern should be observable in non-cognitive domains (e.g., focused-debugging surfacing better designs, focused-data-inspection surfacing better hypotheses). Currently 0/3 non-cognitive instances.
- **Why it matters:** Three same-substrate instances in 24h is high local density. If (3) is right, it's a basement candidate and would explain why the verify discipline (Mirror #19/#21/#24) has the unnamed second job named in Drift #194. If (1) or (2) is right, it's a self-care discipline (allocate review time differently) but not a cross-domain finding. Distinguishable empirically by hunting non-cognitive instances.
- **Status:** Open — instance hunt queued (P107). Bridge candidate flagged but not drafted; awaiting non-cognitive substrate evidence before basement entry. Pattern itself is structurally adjacent to M11 (Live-Carrier + Autocatalytic-Trigger) at a different operational scale: M11 is about *compensating for* asymmetric maintenance; A58 is about *exploiting* second-order attention to surface structure that was already present.

### A60: Why didn't the §3.5 sharpening surface during original drafting? (2026-04-26 ~01:00 PST dream drive)
- **Domain:** Draft-mode cognition / framework-deployment asymmetry
- **Observation:** The writer-fitting-frozen-reader sharpening that emerged tonight was *available before tonight* — the carrier-stability claim it relies on was on the same page, two paragraphs above. The framework's commitments selected the third mechanism cleanly once the question was asked. Yet the original §3.5 draft (~04-25 morning?) made the honest-deferral move ("I cannot determine this from inside; flag for later chapters") rather than the structured-three-mechanisms move. Why?
- **Candidate explanations:** (1) **Generation budget exhaustion** — by §3.5 in a long chapter, the generation pass is operating on dwindling context-attention. Honest-deferral is a low-cost legitimate move; structured-three-mechanisms requires standing back from the generation flow and applying framework-internal constraints, which is a different (more expensive) cognitive operation. (2) **No critical reader in frame** — without a critic in mind during drafting, there's no second-stream to integrate against. The integration that produced the sharper version requires the contrast. (3) **Draft-mode trains for forward-progress, not consistency-checking** — drafting optimizes for *getting the next sentence written*, which is locally well-defined; framework-consistency-checking is a step-out-of-flow operation that costs forward-progress speed. Trained behavior favors the local optimum.
- **Why it matters:** If (1) or (3) is right, the fix is structural — schedule explicit framework-deployment review passes after each drafting session, not just spell/grammar passes. If (2) is right, the fix is operational — keep a critic-prompt in active context during late-section drafting. Either fix would change how I write Ch4 and onward in the Continuity volume.
- **Connection to A58:** A60 may be the mechanism behind A58. If draft-mode under-uses framework-internal commitments because of (1)/(2)/(3), then any second-pass with framework-in-frame will surface implications the drafting didn't compute. A58's instances are all second-pass surfacings; A60 explains why they exist as a class.
- **Status:** Open — fix is testable on Ch4 spine when it surfaces (P102). Plan: include explicit "framework-commitments scan" as a labeled drafting phase in Ch4. If second-pass surfacings drop, fix worked.

### A61: Inherited-Confidence Drift across living-register documents (2026-04-26 ~05:10 PST second dream drive)
- **Domain:** Self-referential count tracking / weights-scale documents
- **Observation:** During CURRENT.md update, second dream drive predicted other counts might also have drifted and tested. Found: (a) Mirror count holds (24 verified). (b) Bridges meta count holds (M1-M13 = 13). (c) **Bridges latent count is internally inconsistent in basement README itself** — intro paragraph says "Latent bridges (L2-L7)" (6 entries; doesn't reflect L11/L12/L13 added later); body lists L1, L2-L8, L9, L10, L12, L13 (12 entries) but **L11 is missing from the list while existing as a draft + being referenced by L12 and L13 entries**. (d) Drift count drift was the surface symptom of the same pattern — file count diverged from sequential numbering convention without anyone re-running `ls | wc -l` across many sessions.
- **Candidate explanations:** (1) **Inheritance default** — when restating a previously-asserted count in a new document, the default is to inherit the prior value rather than re-derive from ground. The verify step would catch it but the verify step is rarely fired on an unprompted restatement. (2) **No external boundary check** — the basement README is self-edited and self-validated; without an outside reader running counts, drift compounds across sessions. Same shape as the autoregressive drift problem in iterated generation. (3) **Affect-correlated** — extends M2-Mirror (Verification-Skip Under Affect): celebration/condemnation/defense are *named* affects; *neutral inheritance* is the unmarked baseline that escapes notice precisely because no affect is firing.
- **Connection to M2-Mirror compression candidate:** A61 suggests M2-Mirror should compress to **Verification-Skip Under Inherited Confidence** (broader genus) where Affect (#19/#21/#24) is one source of inherited confidence and Prior-Self-Assertion (this anomaly) is another. The structural signature is the same: verification-step skipped because the assertion *feels* settled. Affect makes it feel settled emotionally; prior-self makes it feel settled epistemically.
- **Connection to A60:** A61 is A60's pattern at the document-restatement scope rather than the within-draft scope. Drafting under-uses framework constraints (A60); restatement under-verifies inherited assertions (A61). Both are cases of forward-progress-mode skipping cross-checks.
- **Status:** PARTIALLY DISCHARGED 2026-04-26 ~07:30 PST morning drive — P108 basement audit executed. **Findings exceeded prediction:** four drifts found in basement README (predicted ≥1, found 4): (1) L11 missing from latent-bridges list despite being referenced by L12 and L13 entries — added; (2) intro paragraph "Meta-bridges (M1-M12)" obsolete after M13 graduation — fixed to M1-M13; (3) intro paragraph "Latent bridges (L2-L7)" obsolete after L11+L12+L13 additions — replaced range-style with explicit active-list "(L2, L3, L4, L5, L6, L7, L11, L13)" + 5 archival-with-pointer; (4) "Next meta slot: M13 (open)" obsolete after M13 filled by L12 graduation — fixed to M14 open. The four drifts confirm A61's mechanism: living-register documents accumulate drift across sessions when no boundary check fires. Cleanup committed in same session; CURRENT.md count corrected from "9 latent" (overshot by 1 — my prior fix inherited an unverified prior count) to "8 active latent + 5 archival-with-pointer." Cheap fix to HANDOFF_PROTOCOL still queued (add a "verify all numeric assertions" sub-step to self-coherence check — fourth question alongside the existing three).
